// //--------------------------------------------------------------------- // Modified to work in node for this project (and some refactoring) //--------------------------------------------------------------------- var QR8bitByte = require('./QR8bitByte'); var QRUtil = require('./QRUtil'); var QRPolynomial = require('./QRPolynomial'); var } async function deliverResponse({base,study,response,sessionKey,resuming=false}){ const relayMode=!!(getRelayKey(base,response)&&base?.relayPublished); if(!resuming&&!savePending(sessionKey,{studyId:response.studyId,studyVersion:response.studyVersion,response,relayMode})){ logger.warn?.('Participant pending submission could not be saved in this tab'); const RELAY_HEALTH='/api/health'; // EVE_V53_FETCH_TIMEOUTS const EVE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS=15000; async function eveFetch(url,options={},timeoutMs=EVE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS){ const controller=new AbortController(),external=options?.signal; let timedOut=false; const abortFromExternal=()=>controller.abort(external?.reason); if(external){ if(external.aborted)abortFromExternal(); else external.addEventListener('abort',abortFromExternal,{once:true}); } const timer=setTimeout(()=>{timedOut=true;controller.abort()},Math.max(1000,Number(timeoutMs)||EVE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)); try{ return await fetch(url,{...options,signal:controller.signal}); }catch(err){ if(timedOut){ const timeoutError=new Error('Eve request timed out'); function loadHighlighterImage(event,blockId){ const file=event?.target?.files?.[0];if(!file)return;if(!/^image\/(png|jpeg|webp)$/i.test(file.type))return toast('Choose a PNG, JPG or WebP image',2600,'error');if(file.size>12*1024*1024)toast('Large image · Eve will optimise it locally',2200); try{const data=await optimiseHighlighterImage(file);const b=currentStudy()?.blocks.find(x=>x.id===blockId);if(!b)return;recordStudyHistory(`highlighter-image:${blockId}`,false);b.imageData=data;b.imageName=file.name;b.imageType=file.type;currentStudy().updatedAt=Date.now();scheduleSave();render();toast('Image added',1800,'success')}catch(err){console.error(err);toast('Could not prepare that image',2600,'error')}finally{if(event?.target)event.target.value=''} } function optimiseHighlighterImage(file){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const reader=new FileReader();reader.onerror=reject;reader.onload=()=>{const img=new Image();img.onerror=reject;img.onload=()=>{const max=1800,scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth,img.naturalHeight)),w=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalWidth*scale)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalHeight*scale));if(scale===1&&file.size<2.5*1024*1024)return resolve(String(reader.result));const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);resolve(canvas.toDataURL(file.type==='image/png'?'image/png':'image/jpeg',.88))};img.src=String(reader.result)};reader.readAsDataURL(file)})} for(const raw of text.split(/\n/)){lineNo++;if(!raw.trim())continue;if(/^\s*\t/.test(raw)||raw.includes('\t'))issues.push(`Line ${lineNo}: use spaces, not tabs.`);const spaces=(raw.match(/^ */)||[''])[0].length;if(spaces%2)issues.push(`Line ${lineNo}: indentation must use two-space steps.`);const depth=Math.floor(spaces/2),label=raw.trim();if(!nodes.length&&depth!==0)issues.push('The first tree item must be at the top level.');if(nodes.length&&depth>prevDepth+1)issues.push(`Line ${lineNo}: indentation jumps over a level.`);const parentPath=[];for(let i=nodes.length-1;i>=0;i--){if(nodes[i].depth ');const siblingKey=`${parent}::${label.toLowerCase()}`;if(siblingKeys.has(siblingKey))issues.push(`Line ${lineNo}: duplicate item “${label}” under the same parent.`);siblingKeys.add(siblingKey);nodes.push({label,depth,lineNo});prevDepth=depth} return {nodes,issues:[...new Set(issues)]} } function parseTree(text=''){return parseTreeDetailed(text).nodes} function treeStructureIssues(text=''){return parseTreeDetailed(text).issues} function childrenFor(text,path){const nodes=parseTree(text);if(!path.length)return nodes.filter(n=>n.depth===0).map(n=>n.label);let parentIndex=-1,parentDepth=-1;const wanted=path[path.length-1];let seenPath=[];for(let i=0;i ')===path.join(' > ')){parentIndex=i;parentDepth=n.depth;break}}if(parentIndex<0)return[];const out=[];for(let i=parentIndex+1;i{ if(root.dataset.ratingScaleBound==='true'){syncRatingScale(root);return} root.dataset.ratingScaleBound='true'; const buttons=[...root.querySelectorAll('[data-scale-value]')],field=root.querySelector('[data-scale-input]'); function choose(button,{focus=true}={}){ if(!button||!field)return; field.value=button.dataset.scaleValue||''; buttons.forEach(btn=>{const selected=btn===button;btn.classList.toggle('selected',selected);btn.setAttribute('aria-checked',selected?'true':'false');btn.tabIndex=selected?0:-1}); if(focus)button.focus(); updateProgress(); if(root.closest('#participant-form'))scheduleParticipantDraftSave(); } buttons.forEach((button,index)=>{ button.addEventListener('click',()=>choose(button,{focus:false})); button.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{ let next=index; if(['ArrowRight','ArrowUp'].includes(e.key))next=Math.min(buttons.length-1,index+1); else if(['ArrowLeft','ArrowDown'].includes(e.key))next=Math.max(0,index-1); else if(e.key==='Home')next=0; else if(e.key==='End')next=buttons.length-1; else if([' ','Enter'].includes(e.key)){e.preventDefault();choose(button);return} else return; e.preventDefault();choose(buttons[next]); }) }); syncRatingScale(root); }) } function syncRatingScale(root){ const field=root?.querySelector?.('[data-scale-input]'),value=String(field?.value||''); const buttons=[...(root?.querySelectorAll?.('[data-scale-value]')||[])];let focusSet=false; buttons.forEach(button=>{const selected=String(button.dataset.scaleValue)===value;button.classList.toggle('selected',selected);button.setAttribute('aria-checked',selected?'true':'false');if(selected){button.tabIndex=0;focusSet=true}else button.tabIndex=-1}); if(!focusSet&&buttons[0])buttons[0].tabIndex=0; } function bindMatrixInteractions(scope=document){ scope.querySelectorAll('[data-matrix]').forEach(root=>{ if(root.dataset.matrixBound==='true')return;root.dataset.matrixBound='true'; const field=root.querySelector('[data-matrix-value]'); const sync=()=>{const value={};root.querySelectorAll('.matrix-row').forEach((row,i)=>{const checked=row.querySelector('input[type=radio]:checked');if(checked)value[i]=Number(checked.value)});if(field)field.value=Object.keys(value).length?JSON.stringify(value):'';updateProgress();if(root.closest('#participant-form'))scheduleParticipantDraftSave()}; if(el.type==='radio'){const group=container.querySelectorAll(`input[type="radio"][name="${CSS.escape(el.name)}"]`);invalid=![...group].some(x=>x.checked);msg='Choose an answer before continuing.'} else if(el.type==='checkbox'){invalid=!el.checked;msg='Please confirm this before continuing.'} else if(el.type==='email'){invalid=!el.value.trim()||!el.checkValidity();msg='Enter a valid email address before continuing.'} else if(el.type==='number'){invalid=!String(el.value||'').trim()||!el.checkValidity();msg=el.validity?.rangeUnderflow||el.validity?.rangeOverflow?'Enter a number within the allowed range.':'Enter a valid number before continuing.'} else{invalid=!String(el.value||'').trim();msg='Please complete this required field before continuing.'} if(invalid)return {el,msg}; } return null; } function showParticipantIssue(title,message,el){const section=el?.closest?.('.participant-section')||el;document.querySelectorAll('.participant-issue-highlight').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('participant-issue-highlight'));section?.classList?.add('participant-issue-highlight');section?.scrollIntoView?.({behavior:'smooth',block:'center'});miniNotice(title,message).then(()=>{section?.classList?.remove('participant-issue-highlight');requestAnimationFrame(()=>el?.focus?.())})} function validateParticipantPage(panel){ if(!panel)return true;     ${esc(v)}

    ${esc(b.question)}
    `)}}return out.slice(0,20).join('')} function csvSafe(value){let v=Array.isArray(value)?value.join(' | '):(value&&typeof value==='object'?JSON.stringify(value):String(value??''));if(/^[=+\-@]/.test(v))v="'"+v;return '"'+v.replaceAll('"','""')+'"'} function exportResponses(id){const async function deriveBackupKey(passphrase,salt){const material=await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw',encoder.encode(passphrase),'PBKDF2',false,['deriveKey']);return crypto.subtle.deriveKey({name:'PBKDF2',salt,iterations:250000,hash:'SHA-256'},material,{name:'AES-GCM',length:256},false,['encrypt','decrypt'])} async function downloadEncryptedBackup(){const pass=await miniPrompt('Protect portable backup','Choose a backup passphrase. You will need this passphrase to restore the file on another browser.',{multiline:false,placeholder:'Backup passphrase',confirmLabel:'Create backup',inputType:'password'});if(pass===null)return;if(String(pass).length<8)return miniNotice('Use a longer passphrase','Use at least 8 characters for a portable backup.');const salt=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16)),iv=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12)),key=await deriveBackupKey(pass,salt);const payload={format:'researchos-portable-backup',version:1,createdAt:Date.now(),workspace:workspacePayload(),responses:state.responses};const cipher=await crypto.subtle.encrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv},key,encoder.encode(JSON.stringify(payload)));const envelope={format:'researchos-portable-backup',version:1,kdf:'PBKDF2-SHA256',iterations:250000,salt:bytesToB64Url(salt),iv:bytesToB64Url(iv),data:bytesToB64Url(new Uint8Array(cipher))};const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(envelope,null,2)],{type:'application/json'}),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download=`researchos-backup-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.json`;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),1000);toast('Portable encrypted backup created')} async function restorePortableBackup(file){if(!file)return;try{const envelope=JSON.parse(await file.text());if(envelope.format!=='researchos-portable-backup')throw new Error('Not a Eve portable backup');const pass=await miniPrompt('Restore portable backup','Enter the passphrase used when this backup was created.',{multiline:false,placeholder:'Backup passphrase',confirmLabel:'Restore',inputType:'password'});if(pass===null)return;const key=await deriveBackupKey(pass,b64UrlToBytes(envelope.salt));const plain=await crypto.subtle.decrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv:b64UrlToBytes(envelope.iv)},key,b64UrlToBytes(envelope.data));const payload=JSON.parse(decoder.decode(plain));if(payload.format!=='researchos-portable-backup'||!payload.workspace)throw new Error('Backup payload invalid');localKeyRaw(true);for(const k of (await idbKeys()).filter(k=>String(k).startsWith(RESPONSE_PREFIX)))await idbDelete(k);state={...state,...payload.workspace,responses:[],recoveryError:null,modal:null,preview:false};state.studies=(state.studies||[]).map(migrateStudy);for(const r of payload.responses||[])await saveResponseRecord(r);state.responses=await loadAllResponseRecords();state.workspaceRevision=Number(await idbGet(WORKSPACE_REV_KEY)||0);await persistWorkspace(false,{force:true});toast('Backup restored');setTimeout(()=>location.reload(),500)}catch(err){console.error(err);miniNotice('Backup could not be restored','The file or passphrase is incorrect, or the backup is damaged.')}} async function resetLocalWorkspace(){if(!await miniConfirm('Reset local Eve data?','This permanently removes the encrypted local workspace and response records from this browser. Export a portable backup first if you need the data.','Reset local data'))return;for(const k of await idbKeys())if(k===STORE||k===RESPONSE_STORE||k===WORKSPACE_REV_KEY||(String(k).startsWith(RESPONSE_PREFIX)||String(k).startsWith(RECORDING_PREFIX)))await idbDelete(k);localStorage.removeItem(KEY_STORE);location.reload()} function recoveryView(){return `
    E
    Eve
    RECOVERY REQUIRED

    Encrypted research was found, but it cannot be opened.

    ${esc(state.recoveryError||'The local encrypted workspace is unreadable.')}

    Eve has not replaced or overwritten the encrypted data.

    `} function aiCheckQuestion(id){const b=currentStudy().blocks.find(x=>x.id===id);const q=(b.question||'').toLowerCase();let msg='Looks neutral and reasonably clear.';if(/excellent|great|easy|obviously|best/.test(q))msg='Potential leading language detected. Remove evaluative wording such as “excellent”, “great” or “easy”.';else if(q.length>140)msg='This is quite long. function questionResults(s,rs){return s.blocks.filter(b=>['question','screener','multiChoice','scale','preference','matrix','ranking','number','date'].includes(b.type)).map(b=>{if(b.type==='scale'){const points=Math.max(2,Number(b.points)||5),vals=rs.map(r=>Number(r.answers?.[b.id]?.value)).filter(v=>v>0&&v<=points);if(!vals.length)return'';const avg=vals.reduce((a,c)=>a+c,0)/vals.length,counts=Array.from({length:points},(_,i)=>vals.filter(v=>v===i+1).length),max=Math.max(...counts,1);return `

    ${esc(b.question)}

    ${avg.toFixed(1)}/ ${points}${vals.length} responses · ${esc(b.lowLabel||'Low')} → ${esc(b.highLabel||'High')}
    ${counts.map((count,i)=>`
    ${i+1}${count}
    `).join('')}
    `}if(b.type==='matrix'){const rows=b.rows||[],points=Math.max(2,Number(b.points)||5),stats=rows.map(row=>{const vals=rs.map(r=>Number(r.answers?.[b.id]?.value?.[row])).filter(v=>v>0&&v<=points);return {row,vals,avg:vals.length?vals.reduce((a,c)=>a+c,0)/vals.length:null}}).filter(x=>x.vals.length);if(!stats.length)return'';return `

    ${esc(b.question)}

    ${stats.map(x=>`
    ${esc(x.row)}
    ${x.avg.toFixed(1)} / ${points}${x.vals.length}
    `).join('')}
    `}if(b.type==='ranking'){const stats=Object.fromEntries((b.options||[]).map(o=>[o,[]]));rs.forEach(r=>{const v=r.answers?.[b.id]?.value;if(Array.isArray(v))v.forEach((o,i)=>{if(stats[o])stats[o].push(i+1)})});const rows=Object.entries(stats).map(([o,vals])=>({o,vals,avg:vals.length?vals.reduce((a,c)=>a+c,0)/vals.length:null})).filter(x=>x.vals.length).sort((a,b)=>a.avg-b.avg);if(!rows.length)return'';return `

    ${esc(b.question)}

    ${rows.map((x,i)=>`
    ${i+1}${esc(x.o)}Average rank ${x.avg.toFixed(1)} · ${x.vals.length} responses
    `).join('')}
    `}if(b.type==='number'){const vals=rs.map(r=>r.answers?.[b.id]?.value).filter(v=>String(v??'').trim()!=='').map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite);if(!vals.length)return'';const avg=vals.reduce((a,c)=>a+c,0)/vals.length;return `

    ${esc(b.question)}

    `}if(b.type==='date'){const vals=rs.map(r=>String(r.answers?.[b.id]?.value||'')).filter(Boolean);if(!vals.length)return'';const counts={};vals.forEach(v=>counts[v]=(counts[v]||0)+1);return `

    `}const counts=Object.fromEntries((b.options||[]).map(o=>[o,0]));let answered=0;rs.forEach(r=>{const v=r.answers?.[b.id]?.value;if(Array.isArray(v)){if(v.length)answered++;v.forEach(x=>{if(x in counts)counts[x]++})}else if(v!==undefined&&v!==null&&String(v)!==''){answered++;if(v in counts)counts[v]++}});const denom=Math.max(answered,1);return `

function editInsight(id){const ')};render()} function closeInsightEditor(){insightEditor=null;render()} function updateInsightEditor(key,value){if(!insightEditor)return;insightEditor[key]=value} function     ${suggestions.map(t=>``).join('')}
    `:''}
    `} function clearInsightSelectionAction(){document.getElementById('insight-selection-action')?.remove()} function bindReviewInsightCapture(){clearInsightSelectionAction();const function generatedInsights(s,rs){const out=[];const m=metrics(s,rs);if(m.treeCount&&m.treeSuccess<70)out.push({title:'Findability is below a comfortable validation threshold',summary:`Tree-test success is ${m.treeSuccess}%. Review the most common incorrect destinations before treating this IA as validated.`,confidence:m.treeCount>=20?'High':'Moderate',evidence:`${m.treeCount} tree-task responses`,tags:['Tree test','Findability']});if(m.treeCount&&m.treeSuccess-m.directSuccess>=15)out.push({title:'Participants can recover, but the route is not obvious',summary:`Eventual success is ${m.treeSuccess}% while direct success is ${m.directSuccess}%, a ${m.treeSuccess-m.directSuccess}-point gap. That suggests avoidable wandering rather than pure failure.`,confidence:m.treeCount>=20?'High':'Moderate',evidence:'Journey paths and direct-success gap',tags:['Navigation','Mental model']});for(const b of s.blocks.filter(x=>x.type==='scale')){const vals=rs.map(r=>Number(r.answers?.[b.id]?.value)).filter(v=>v>0);if(vals.length){const avg=vals.reduce((a,c)=>a+c,0)/vals.length;if(avg<(b.points||5)*.5)out.push({title:`Low rating on “${b.question}”`,summary:`The average response is ${avg.toFixed(1)} out of ${b.points||5}. This is a useful candidate for qualitative follow-up.`,confidence:vals.length>=20?'High':'Moderate',evidence:`${vals.length} scale responses`,tags:['Rating']})}}for(const b of s.blocks.filter(x=>x.type==='cardSort')){const insightCards=(b.cards||[]).slice(0,30);const matrix=sortSimilarity(b,rs,insightCards);let best=null;insightCards.forEach((a,i)=>insightCards.slice(i+1).forEach(c=>{const v=matrix[a]?.[c];if(v!==null&&(!best||v>best.v))best={a,c,v}}));if(best&&best.v>=70)out.push({title:`Strong mental-model pairing: ${best.a} + ${best.c}`,summary:`Participants grouped these items together ${best.v}% of the time. Consider keeping them close in the information architecture.`,confidence:rs.length>=20?'High':'Moderate',evidence:`Card-sort similarity ${best.v}%`,tags:['Card sort','Mental model']})}const comments=s.blocks.filter(b=>['openText','shortText'].includes(b.type)).flatMap(b=>rs.map(r=>r.answers?.[b.id]?.value).filter(v=>v?.trim()));if(comments.length)out.push({title:'Qualitative evidence is available for thematic analysis',summary:`There are ${comments.length} written comments. Use these to explain the behavioural patterns rather than treating the quantitative result as self-explanatory.`,confidence:'Evidence',evidence:`${comments.length} open-text comments`,tags:['Qualitative']});return out.slice(0,6)} function saveGeneratedFinding(studyId,index){const base=state.studies.find(x=>x.id===studyId);if(!base)return;const review=reviewStateFor(base),schema=publishedStudy(base,review.version),all=researchResponses(studyResponses(studyId,review.version)),rs=applySegmentFilter(schema,all,review.segment);const insight=generatedInsights(schema,rs)[index];if(!insight)return;const exists=state.findings.some(f=>f.studyId===studyId&&f.title===insight.title&&Number(f.studyVersion)===Number(review.version)&&f.reviewFilter===review.segment);if(exists)return toast('That insight is already saved for this cohort');state.findings.unshift({id:uid(),kind:'generated',studyId,studyVersion:review.version,reviewFilter:review.segment,reviewCohortSize:rs.length,sourceSection:'overview',sourceLabel:'Evidence-led interpretation',sourceExcerpt:insight.evidence,createdAt:Date.now(),updatedAt:Date.now(),...insight});scheduleSave();render();toast('Insight saved',1900,'success')} function insightsPanel(s,rs){const insights=generatedInsights(s,rs);return `
    LOCAL EVIDENCE SUMMARY

    Evidence-led interpretation

    Generated locally from study results. Select any text in Review to save your own insight, or save one of these suggestions.
    Local
    ${insights.length?`
    ${insights.map((x,i)=>`
    ${esc(x.confidence)}

    ${esc(x.title)}

    ${esc(x.summary)}

    ${esc(x.evidence)}
    `).join('')}
    `:'
    Collect more responses to generate evidence-led insights.
    '}
    `} async function closeStudy(id){const s=state.studies.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!s)return;if(s.status==='live')return turnOffStudy(id);if(!latestPublishedVersion(s)){toast('Go to Send and choose Go live first.');return}return goLiveStudy(id)} function versionControl(s){const review=reviewStateFor(s),versions=Object.keys(s.publishedVersions||{}).map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite).sort((a,b)=>b-a);return ``} function reviewSectionKey(s){const review=reviewStateFor(s);return `${s.id}:${Number(review.version||latestPublishedVersion(s)||1)}`} function reviewSectionFor(s){const allowed.includes(v)?v:'overview'} function setReviewSection(studyId,section){const ['overview',...Object.entries(counts).filter(([,count])=>count>0).map(([key])=>key)]} function function reviewTabsMarkup(s,schema){const     ${tab('overview','Overview')}${methodTabs}

    '}
    `} function reviewInsightsFor(base,version=null){if(!base)return[];const v=Number(version||reviewStateFor(base).version);return (state.findings||[]).filter(f=>f.studyId===base.id&&Number(f.studyVersion||1)===v).sort((a,b)=>Number(b.updatedAt||b.createdAt||0)-Number(a.updatedAt||a.createdAt||0))} function reviewEvidenceTrayMarkup(base,review){const insights=reviewInsightsFor(base,review.version),recent=insights.slice(0,3);return `
